Motivation: 传统单机吊运系统存在载荷能力和控制权限限制，现有多机吊运研究依赖GPS或集中式架构，缺乏传感器失效鲁棒性，无法在GPS拒止或受限环境中工作

Method: 使用机载单目相机检测载荷上的基准标记并估计相对位姿，通过分布式去中心化扩展信息滤波器融合测量数据，实现闭环轨迹跟踪控制

Result: Gazebo中的蒙特卡洛仿真结果表明该方法有效，包括飞行过程中通信丢失情况下的性能表现

Conclusion: 提出的分布式视觉多机吊运框架为GPS拒止环境下的鲁棒、可扩展多机协同吊运提供了可行解决方案

Abstract: Transporting heavy or oversized slung loads using rotorcraft has traditionally relied on single-aircraft systems, which limits both payload capacity and control authority. Cooperative multilift using teams of rotorcraft offers a scalable and efficient alternative, especially for infrequent but challenging "long-tail" payloads without the need of building larger and larger rotorcraft. Most prior multilift research assumes GPS availability, uses centralized estimation architectures, or relies on controlled laboratory motion-capture setups. As a result, these methods lack robustness to sensor loss and are not viable in GPS-denied or operationally constrained environments. This paper addresses this limitation by presenting a distributed and decentralized payload state estimation framework for vision-based multilift operations. Using onboard monocular cameras, each UAV detects a fiducial marker on the payload and estimates its relative pose. These measurements are fused via a Distributed and Decentralized Extended Information Filter (DDEIF), enabling robust and scalable estimation that is resilient to individual sensor dropouts. This payload state estimate is then used for closed-loop trajectory tracking control. Monte Carlo simulation results in Gazebo show the effectiveness of the proposed approach, including the effect of communication loss during flight.

</details>


### [191] [Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04579)
*Michael Groom,James Wilson,Nick Hawes,Lars Kunze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个基于模仿学习的风险感知视觉运动策略，用于移动机械臂在未知环境中进行全身控制，通过分布强化学习和风险度量实现可调节的风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动机械臂全身控制器缺乏在不确定性下进行风险敏感决策的机制，而机器人从实验室过渡到日常环境需要能够评估行动风险并做出风险感知决策。

Method: 首先使用分布强化学习训练特权教师策略，采用风险中性分布评论家；应用扭曲风险度量到评论家预测的回报分布，计算风险调整优势估计用于策略更新；然后通过模仿学习将教师策略蒸馏为基于自我中心深度观测的风险感知学生策略。

Result: 训练出的视觉运动策略在未映射环境中表现出风险感知行为（特别是获得更好的最坏情况性能），同时执行反应性全身运动，利用实时深度观测进行感知。

Conclusion: 首次实现了基于自我中心深度观测、运行时可调节风险敏感性的风险感知视觉运动策略学习，并通过模仿学习成功转移风险感知行为，为移动机械臂在动态非结构化空间中的安全操作提供了新方法。

Abstract: For robots to successfully transition from lab settings to everyday environments, they must begin to reason about the risks associated with their actions and make informed, risk-aware decisions. This is particularly true for robots performing mobile manipulation tasks, which involve both interacting with and navigating within dynamic, unstructured spaces. However, existing whole-body controllers for mobile manipulators typically lack explicit mechanisms for risk-sensitive decision-making under uncertainty. To our knowledge, we are the first to (i) learn risk-aware visuomotor policies for mobile manipulation conditioned on egocentric depth observations with runtime-adjustable risk sensitivity, and (ii) show risk-aware behaviours can be transferred through Imitation Learning (IL) to a visuomotor policy conditioned on egocentric depth observations. Our method achieves this by first training a privileged teacher policy using Distributional Reinforcement Learning (DRL), with a risk-neutral distributional critic. Distortion risk-metrics are then applied to the critic's predicted return distribution to calculate risk-adjusted advantage estimates used in policy updates to achieve a range of risk-aware behaviours. We then distil teacher policies with IL to obtain risk-aware student policies conditioned on egocentric depth observations. We perform extensive evaluations demonstrating that our trained visuomotor policies exhibit risk-aware behaviour (specifically achieving better worst-case performance) while performing reactive whole-body motions in unmapped environments, leveraging live depth observations for perception.

</details>


### [192] [ELLIPSE: Evidential Learning for Robust Waypoints and Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04585)
*Zihao Dong,Chanyoung Chung,Dong-Ki Kim,Mukhtar Maulimov,Xiangyun Meng,Harmish Khambhaita,Ali-akbar Agha-mohammadi,Amirreza Shaban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELLIPSE：基于深度证据回归的鲁棒航点预测方法，通过多元Student-t分布输出预测不确定性，采用领域增强和校准技术提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在开放世界安全关键场景中需要鲁棒的航点预测。虽然模仿学习方法在实践中表现出色，但容易受到分布偏移的影响，导致在陌生状态下过度自信，存在安全隐患。

Method: 1. 基于多元深度证据回归输出航点和多元Student-t预测分布；2. 引入轻量级领域增强程序，合成合理的视点/姿态变化以减少协变量偏移引起的过度自信；3. 应用后验等渗重新校准PIT值，提升环境/领域偏移下的不确定性可靠性

Result: 在真实世界评估中，ELLIPSE相比基线方法在任务成功率和不确定性覆盖方面均有显著提升，特别在楼梯航点预测任务中表现出色

Conclusion: ELLIPSE通过结合深度证据回归、领域增强和校准技术，为移动机器人提供了鲁棒的航点预测和可靠的不确定性估计，在分布偏移场景下表现出优越性能

Abstract: Robust waypoint prediction is crucial for mobile robots operating in open-world, safety-critical settings. While Imitation Learning (IL) methods have demonstrated great success in practice, they are susceptible to distribution shifts: the policy can become dangerously overconfident in unfamiliar states. In this paper, we present \textit{ELLIPSE}, a method building on multivariate deep evidential regression to output waypoints and multivariate Student-t predictive distributions in a single forward pass. To reduce covariate-shift-induced overconfidence under viewpoint and pose perturbations near expert trajectories, we introduce a lightweight domain augmentation procedure that synthesizes plausible viewpoint/pose variations without collecting additional demonstrations. To improve uncertainty reliability under environment/domain shift (e.g., unseen staircases), we apply a post-hoc isotonic recalibration on probability integral transform (PIT) values so that prediction sets remain plausible during deployment. We ground the discussion and experiments in staircase waypoint prediction, where obtaining robust waypoint and uncertainty is pivotal. Extensive real world evaluations show that \textit{ELLIPSE} improves both task success rate and uncertainty coverage compared to baselines.

</details>


### [193] [RoboMME: Benchmarking and Understanding Memory for Robotic Generalist Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04639)
*Yinpei Dai,Hongze Fu,Jayjun Lee,Yuejiang Liu,Haoran Zhang,Jianing Yang,Chelsea Finn,Nima Fazeli,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboMME：一个用于评估视觉-语言-动作模型在长时程、历史依赖场景中记忆能力的大规模标准化基准，包含16个任务和14种记忆增强变体


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆对于长时程和历史依赖的机器人操作至关重要，但现有VLA模型的记忆机制评估局限于狭窄、非标准化的设置，限制了系统理解、比较和进展测量

Method: 提出RoboMME基准，包含16个在精心设计分类法下构建的操作任务，评估时间、空间、对象和程序记忆；基于π0.5骨干开发了14种记忆增强VLA变体，系统探索不同记忆表示和集成策略

Result: 实验结果表明记忆表示的有效性高度依赖于任务，每种设计在不同任务中都有独特的优势和局限性

Conclusion: RoboMME基准为VLA模型的记忆能力评估提供了标准化框架，揭示了记忆表示的任务依赖性，为未来研究提供了系统化的评估平台

Abstract: Memory is critical for long-horizon and history-dependent robotic manipulation. Such tasks often involve counting repeated actions or manipulating objects that become temporarily occluded. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate memory mechanisms; however, their evaluations remain confined to narrow, non-standardized settings. This limits their systematic understanding, comparison, and progress measurement. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboMME: a large-scale standardized benchmark for evaluating and advancing VLA models in long-horizon, history-dependent scenarios. Our benchmark comprises 16 manipulation tasks constructed under a carefully designed taxonomy that evaluates temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. We further develop a suite of 14 memory-augmented VLA variants built on the π0.5 backbone to systematically explore different memory representations across multiple integration strategies. Experimental results show that the effectiveness of memory representations is highly task-dependent, with each design offering distinct advantages and limitations across different tasks. Videos and code can be found at our website https://robomme.github.io.

</details>


### [194] [Autonomous Aerial Non-Destructive Testing: Ultrasound Inspection with a Commercial Quadrotor in an Unstructured Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04642)
*Ruben Veenstra,Barbara Bazzana,Sander Smits,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个完全自主的基于接触式无损检测系统，通过改造商用多旋翼无人机实现自主物理交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用多旋翼无人机通常仅限于遥控操作，无法实现自主的基于接触的物理交互，特别是在危险和受限基础设施的无损检测领域存在需求

Method: 开发实时控制软件架构，直接与无人机机载传感器套件接口；采用多速率控制方案：底层控制200Hz，力估计100Hz，导纳滤波器和轨迹规划器50Hz；最终向内部飞行控制器提供加速度和偏航率命令

Result: 在配备超声波有效载荷的Flyability Elios 3四旋翼无人机上进行物理实验验证；系统完全依赖机载传感，在非结构化工业环境中成功执行自主无损检测测量

Conclusion: 证明了改造现成平台实现自主物理交互的可行性，为安全、基于接触的危险和受限基础设施检测铺平了道路

Abstract: This work presents an integrated control and software architecture that enables arguably the first fully autonomous, contact-based non-destructive testing (NDT) using a commercial multirotor originally restricted to remotely-piloted operations. To allow autonomous operation with an off-the-shelf platform, we developed a real-time framework that interfaces directly with its onboard sensor suite. The architecture features a multi-rate control scheme: low-level control is executed at 200 Hz, force estimation at 100 Hz, while an admittance filter and trajectory planner operate at 50 Hz, ultimately supplying acceleration and yaw rate commands to the internal flight controller. We validate the system through physical experiments on a Flyability Elios 3 quadrotor equipped with an ultrasound payload. Relying exclusively on onboard sensing, the vehicle successfully performs autonomous NDT measurements within an unstructured, industrial-like environment. This work demonstrates the viability of retrofitting off-the-shelf platforms for autonomous physical interaction, paving the way for safe, contact-based inspection of hazardous and confined infrastructure.

</details>


### [195] [Python Bindings for a Large C++ Robotics Library: The Case of OMPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04668)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用LLM辅助生成C++库的Python绑定，通过人类专家审查确保正确性，在运动规划库案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python绑定是连接高性能C++库和Python灵活性的关键桥梁，但为大型代码库生成绑定是一个繁琐的过程，给少数维护者带来沉重负担。

Method: 提出基于LLM的工作流程：镜像C++代码库结构，搭建空包装文件框架，使用LLM填充绑定定义，然后由专家审查和优化生成的代码以确保正确性、兼容性和性能。

Result: 在大型C++运动规划库的案例研究中，记录了常见失败模式（共享指针管理、重载、trampoline等），展示了上下文示例和精心设计的提示如何提高可靠性。实验表明生成的绑定在运行时性能上与遗留解决方案相当。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅适用于特定案例，还为在大型C++项目中应用LLM进行绑定生成提供了通用经验教训，证明了LLM辅助生成结合人类专家审查的可行性。

Abstract: Python bindings are a critical bridge between high-performance C++ libraries and the flexibility of Python, enabling rapid prototyping, reproducible experiments, and integration with simulation and learning frameworks in robotics research. Yet, generating bindings for large codebases is a tedious process that creates a heavy burden for a small group of maintainers. In this work, we investigate the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist in generating nanobind wrappers, with human experts kept in the loop. Our workflow mirrors the structure of the C++ codebase, scaffolds empty wrapper files, and employs LLMs to fill in binding definitions. Experts then review and refine the generated code to ensure correctness, compatibility, and performance. Through a case study on a large C++ motion planning library, we document common failure modes, including mismanaging shared pointers, overloads, and trampolines, and show how in-context examples and careful prompt design improve reliability. Experiments demonstrate that the resulting bindings achieve runtime performance comparable to legacy solutions. Beyond this case study, our results provide general lessons for applying LLMs to binding generation in large-scale C++ projects.

</details>


### [196] [LEGS-POMDP: Language and Gesture-Guided Object Search in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04705)
*Ivy Xiao He,Stefanie Tellex,Jason Xinyu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LEGS-POMDP：一种用于开放世界物体搜索的模块化POMDP系统，整合语言、手势和视觉观察，显式建模目标物体身份和空间位置的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在开放世界环境中需要解释模糊指令来定位目标物体。基于基础模型的方法擅长多模态接地，但缺乏建模长时任务不确定性的原则性机制；而POMDPs虽然提供系统化不确定性规划框架，但模态支持有限且依赖限制性环境假设。

Method: 提出LEGS-POMDP（语言和手势引导的部分可观测环境物体搜索POMDP），这是一个模块化POMDP系统，整合语言、手势和视觉观察。系统显式建模两种部分可观测性来源：目标物体身份的不确定性和空间位置的不确定性。

Result: 在仿真中，多模态融合显著优于单模态基线，在挑战性环境和物体类别中平均成功率达到89%。在四足移动机械臂上的真实世界实验定性地验证了鲁棒的多模态感知和模糊指令下的不确定性降低。

Conclusion: LEGS-POMDP成功地将基础模型的多模态能力与POMDPs的原则性不确定性建模相结合，为开放世界物体搜索提供了有效的解决方案，在仿真和真实世界中都表现出色。

Abstract: To assist humans in open-world environments, robots must interpret ambiguous instructions to locate desired objects. Foundation model-based approaches excel at multimodal grounding, but they lack a principled mechanism for modeling uncertainty in long-horizon tasks. In contrast, Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a systematic framework for planning under uncertainty but are often limited in supported modalities and rely on restrictive environment assumptions. We introduce LanguagE and Gesture-Guided Object Search in Partially Observable Environments (LEGS-POMDP), a modular POMDP system that integrates language, gesture, and visual observations for open-world object search. Unlike prior work, LEGS-POMDP explicitly models two sources of partial observability: uncertainty over the target object's identity and its spatial location. In simulation, multimodal fusion significantly outperforms unimodal baselines, achieving an average success rate of 89\% across challenging environments and object categories. Finally, we demonstrate the full system on a quadruped mobile manipulator, where real-world experiments qualitatively validate robust multimodal perception and uncertainty reduction under ambiguous instructions.

</details>


### [197] [Design, Mapping, and Contact Anticipation with 3D-printed Whole-Body Tactile and Proximity Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04714)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Anna Soukhovei,Caleb Escobedo,Nataliya Nechyporenko,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenTact-Prox是一种集成了触觉和接近感应的3D打印人工皮肤，可实现接触检测和预测，感知范围达18厘米，并引入了用于映射机器人周围感知空间的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在动态和共享环境中运行时，需要在接触发生前进行预测，这有助于提高操作安全性和效率。

Method: 开发了模块化设计的GenTact-Prox人工皮肤平台，采用3D打印技术，可程序化生成以适应任何机器人形态；引入了数据驱动的框架来映射感知空间，并在Franka Research 3机器人上部署了五个单元进行验证。

Result: 人工皮肤实现了高达18厘米的检测范围；成功演示了在线物体感知操作和接触预测能力。

Conclusion: GenTact-Prox平台为机器人提供了接触检测和预测能力，通过感知空间映射框架增强了机器人对周围环境的感知，提高了在动态环境中的操作能力。

Abstract: Robots operating in dynamic and shared environments benefit from anticipating contact before it occurs. We present GenTact-Prox, a fully 3D-printed artificial skin that integrates tactile and proximity sensing for contact detection and anticipation. The artificial skin platform is modular in design, procedurally generated to fit any robot morphology, and can cover the whole body of a robot. The skin achieved detection ranges of up to 18 cm during evaluation. To characterize how robots perceive nearby space through this skin, we introduce a data-driven framework for mapping the Perisensory Space -- the body-centric volume of space around the robot where sensors provide actionable information for contact anticipation. We demonstrate this approach on a Franka Research 3 robot equipped with five GenTact-Prox units, enabling online object-aware operation and contact prediction.

</details>


### [198] [Gait Generation Balancing Joint Load and Mobility for Legged Modular Robots with Easily Detachable Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04757)
*Kennosuke Chihara,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于NSGA-III算法的优化框架，在保持必要运动速度和稳定性的同时最小化关节负载，解决模块化机器人运动中的机械失效风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人虽然具有多功能性，但在运动过程中关节扭矩过大会显著增加机械失效风险，特别是对于可拆卸关节。传统方法仅优先考虑移动性，而忽视了关节负载问题。

Method: 使用NSGA-III（非支配排序遗传算法III）优化框架，通过多目标优化获得帕累托最优解，在最小化关节负载的同时保持必要的运动速度和稳定性。

Result: 仿真和物理实验表明，该方法成功为不同环境（如斜坡和台阶）生成步态运动，确保结构完整性而不损害整体移动性。

Conclusion: 提出的优化框架有效解决了模块化机器人运动中的关节负载问题，通过多目标优化平衡了结构完整性和移动性需求，为模块化机器人在复杂环境中的可靠运动提供了解决方案。

Abstract: While modular robots offer versatility, excessive joint torque during locomotion poses a significant risk of mechanical failure, especially for detachable joints. To address this, we propose an optimization framework using the NSGA-III algorithm. Unlike conventional approaches that prioritize mobility alone, our method derives Pareto optimal solutions to minimize joint load while maintaining necessary locomotion speed and stability. Simulations and physical experiments demonstrate that our approach successfully generates gait motions for diverse environments, such as slopes and steps, ensuring structural integrity without compromising overall mobility.

</details>


### [199] [Designing and Validating a Self-Aligning Tool Changer for Modular Reconfigurable Manipulation Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04760)
*Mahfudz Maskur,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种容错工具更换系统，通过被动自对准几何结构补偿定位误差，实现模块化可重构机器人的可靠自主工具交换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性主动耦合机制由于不可避免的定位和方向误差而经常失效，模块化可重构机器人需要可靠的自动化模块交换机制

Method: 采用电机驱动耦合机构，利用被动自对准几何结构（倒角插座和三角形导入导轨）来补偿角度和横向错位，无需复杂力传感器，并配备紧凑旋转工具交换站进行高效模块存储

Result: 实际自主工具拾取实验验证了自对准特性成功吸收执行误差，实现了高度可靠的机器人工具重构

Conclusion: 提出的容错工具更换系统通过被动自对准机制有效解决了模块化机器人自主工具交换中的定位误差问题，提高了系统可靠性

Abstract: Modular reconfigurable robots require reliable mechanisms for automated module exchange, but conventional rigid active couplings often fail due to inevitable positioning and orientational errors. To address this, we propose a misalignment-tolerant tool-changing system. The hardware features a motor-driven coupling utilizing passive self-alignment geometries, specifically chamfered receptacles and triangular lead-in guides, to robustly compensate for angular and lateral misalignments without complex force sensors. To make this autonomous exchange practically feasible, the mechanism is complemented by a compact rotating tool exchange station for efficient module storage. Real-world autonomous tool-picking experiments validate that the self-aligning features successfully absorb execution errors, enabling highly reliable robotic tool reconfiguration.

</details>


### [200] [Adaptive Policy Switching of Two-Wheeled Differential Robots for Traversing over Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04761)
*Haruki Izawa,Takeshi Takai,Shingo Kitano,Mikita Miyaguchi,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于机器人姿态观测进行地形分类，为自适应策略切换提供基础


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球熔岩管探测需要机器人自主导航，预训练策略无法覆盖所有地形条件，需要实现基于当前地形特征的自适应策略切换

Method: 使用PPO微调预训练策略，在模拟熔岩管环境中收集机器人在平坦和粗糙地形上的3D姿态数据，分析俯仰角标准差的地形差异，采用高斯混合模型评估不同窗口大小的地形分类性能

Result: 机器人俯仰角标准差在两种地形类型间存在明显差异，使用70步窗口时地形分类准确率超过98%

Conclusion: 短期姿态数据足以实现可靠的地形估计，为自适应策略切换提供了可行基础

Abstract: Exploring lunar lava tubes requires robots to traverse without human intervention. Because pre-trained policies cannot fully cover all possible terrain conditions, our goal is to enable adaptive policy switching, where the robot selects an appropriate terrain-specialized model based on its current terrain features. This study investigates whether terrain types can be estimated effectively using posture-related observations collected during navigation. We fine-tuned a pre-trained policy using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and then collected the robot's 3D orientation data as it moved across flat and rough terrain in a simulated lava-tube environment. Our analysis revealed that the standard deviation of the robot's pitch data shows a clear difference between these two terrain types. Using Gaussian mixture models (GMM), we evaluated terrain classification across various window sizes. An accuracy of more than 98% was achieved when using a 70-step window. The result suggests that short-term orientation data are sufficient for reliable terrain estimation, providing a foundation for adaptive policy switching.

</details>


### [201] [LLM-Guided Decentralized Exploration with Self-Organizing Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04762)
*Hiroaki Kawashima,Shun Ikejima,Takeshi Takai,Mikita Miyaguchi,Yasuharu Kunii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多机器人探索方法，结合自组织团队形成算法和基于大语言模型的自主目标确定策略，实现去中心化的高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个机器人感知能力有限且容错性不足，需要多机器人组队以扩大观测范围和提升可靠性。传统方法依赖中央控制器，但从鲁棒性和灵活性角度考虑，需要实现去中心化的自主集群操作。在多团队探索场景中，为每个团队确定探索目标对效率至关重要。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：(1) 自组织算法实现自主动态的多团队形成；(2) 基于大语言模型的算法让每个团队自主确定下一个探索目标（目的地）。该方法对比了经典前沿方法和深度强化学习方法。

Result: 通过涉及数十到数百个机器人的仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合自组织团队形成和基于LLM的目标确定策略，能够实现去中心化的高效多机器人探索，解决了传统中央控制方法的鲁棒性和灵活性不足问题。

Abstract: When individual robots have limited sensing capabilities or insufficient fault tolerance, it becomes necessary for multiple robots to form teams during exploration, thereby increasing the collective observation range and reliability. Traditionally, swarm formation has often been managed by a central controller; however, from the perspectives of robustness and flexibility, it is preferable for the swarm to operate autonomously even in the absence of centralized control. In addition, the determination of exploration targets for each team is crucial for efficient exploration in such multi-team exploration scenarios. This study therefore proposes an exploration method that combines (1) an algorithm for self-organization, enabling the autonomous and dynamic formation of multiple teams, and (2) an algorithm that allows each team to autonomously determine its next exploration target (destination). In particular, for (2), this study explores a novel strategy based on large language models (LLMs), while classical frontier-based methods and deep reinforcement learning approaches have been widely studied. The effectiveness of the proposed method was validated through simulations involving tens to hundreds of robots.

</details>


### [202] [On the Strengths and Weaknesses of Data for Open-set Embodied Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04819)
*Pradyumna Tambwekar,Andrew Silva,Deepak Gopinath,Jonathan DeCastro,Xiongyi Cui,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在合成领域通过多样化交互辅助数据微调多模态基础模型，以提升其在开放集纠正辅助任务中的泛化能力，特别是在未见过的用户行为类别和新配置场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型在机器人、自动驾驶等现实领域表现优异，但在交互或辅助场景中，模型需要能够泛化到新用户和新任务。现有方法通常假设封闭的纠正类别或依赖外部规划器，无法解决开放集纠正辅助任务，这成为评估辅助数据极限的挑战性测试平台。

Method: 在Overcooked游戏中生成合成辅助数据集，并基于LLaMA模型进行微调。研究模型在两个维度上的泛化能力：a) 对未见过的用户行为类别提供辅助；b) 在训练中未遇到的新配置场景中提供指导。任务定义为开放集纠正辅助，要求模型检查冗长的用户行为并通过纠正动作或语言反馈提供帮助。

Result: 研究表明，高性能模型受益于覆盖辅助不同方面的数据集，包括多模态接地、缺陷推断以及多样化场景的暴露。该方法为理解实现开放集辅助智能所需的辅助数据集性质提供了关键见解。

Conclusion: 多样化交互数据生成为交互式具身基础模型的数据高效泛化能力提供了有前景的途径。通过合成辅助数据集和模型微调，该研究揭示了实现开放集辅助智能所需的数据集特征，为未来具身辅助系统的发展提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are increasingly performant in real-world domains such as robotics or autonomous driving. These models are often deployed in interactive or assistive settings, where it is important that these assistive models generalize to new users and new tasks. Diverse interactive data generation offers a promising avenue for providing data-efficient generalization capabilities for interactive embodied foundation models. In this paper, we investigate the generalization capabilities of a multimodal foundation model fine-tuned on diverse interactive assistance data in a synthetic domain. We explore generalization along two axes: a) assistance with unseen categories of user behavior and b) providing guidance in new configurations not encountered during training. We study a broad capability called \textbf{Open-Set Corrective Assistance}, in which the model needs to inspect lengthy user behavior and provide assistance through either corrective actions or language-based feedback. This task remains unsolved in prior work, which typically assumes closed corrective categories or relies on external planners, making it a challenging testbed for evaluating the limits of assistive data. To support this task, we generate synthetic assistive datasets in Overcooked and fine-tune a LLaMA-based model to evaluate generalization to novel tasks and user behaviors. Our approach provides key insights into the nature of assistive datasets required to enable open-set assistive intelligence. In particular, we show that performant models benefit from datasets that cover different aspects of assistance, including multimodal grounding, defect inference, and exposure to diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [203] [Task-Relevant and Irrelevant Region-Aware Augmentation for Generalizable Vision-Based Imitation Learning in Agricultural Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04845)
*Shun Hattori,Hikaru Sasaki,Takumi Hachimine,Yusuke Mizutani,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAIL提出区域感知增强框架，通过分离任务相关与无关区域进行针对性增强，提升农业机器人视觉模仿学习的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的模仿学习在农业机器人操作中泛化能力有限，主要由于演示数据稀缺以及作物外观多样性和背景变化导致的视觉域差距

Method: DRAIL框架将视觉观察分离为任务相关区域和任务无关区域：任务相关区域采用领域知识驱动增强以保留关键视觉特征，任务无关区域进行激进随机化以抑制虚假背景相关性

Result: 在人工蔬菜收获和真实生菜缺陷叶片采摘任务中，DRAIL在未见视觉条件下相比基线方法持续提升成功率；注意力分析和表示泛化指标表明学习策略更依赖任务本质视觉特征

Conclusion: DRAIL通过区域感知增强有效处理视觉变化，使学习策略依赖任务本质特征而非偶然视觉线索，增强了农业机器人视觉模仿学习的鲁棒性和泛化能力

Abstract: Vision-based imitation learning has shown promise for robotic manipulation; however, its generalization remains limited in practical agricultural tasks. This limitation stems from scarce demonstration data and substantial visual domain gaps caused by i) crop-specific appearance diversity and ii) background variations. To address this limitation, we propose Dual-Region Augmentation for Imitation Learning (DRAIL), a region-aware augmentation framework designed for generalizable vision-based imitation learning in agricultural manipulation. DRAIL explicitly separates visual observations into task-relevant and task-irrelevant regions. The task-relevant region is augmented in a domain-knowledge-driven manner to preserve essential visual characteristics, while the task-irrelevant region is aggressively randomized to suppress spurious background correlations. By jointly handling both sources of visual variation, DRAIL promotes learning policies that rely on task-essential features rather than incidental visual cues. We evaluate DRAIL on diffusion policy-based visuomotor controllers through robot experiments on artificial vegetable harvesting and real lettuce defective leaf picking preparation tasks. The results show consistent improvements in success rates under unseen visual conditions compared to baseline methods. Further attention analysis and representation generalization metrics indicate that the learned policies rely more on task-essential visual features, resulting in enhanced robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [204] [Hyperbolic Multiview Pretraining for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04848)
*Jin Yang,Ping Wei,Yixin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyperMVP：一种用于机器人操作的自监督双曲多视图预训练框架，利用双曲空间的几何特性学习结构化嵌入，在多种任务和扰动设置中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D感知视觉预训练方法局限于欧几里得嵌入空间，其平坦几何特性限制了建模嵌入间结构关系的能力，难以学习机器人应用中鲁棒空间感知所需的结构化嵌入。

Method: 提出HyperMVP自监督框架，扩展掩码自编码器范式，设计GeoLink编码器学习多视图双曲表示，使用3D-MOV大规模多类型点云数据集支持预训练，然后将预训练编码器与视觉运动策略在操作任务上进行微调。

Result: 在COLOSSEUM、RLBench和真实场景评估中，HyperMVP在多样化任务和扰动设置中一致优于强基线方法，展示了在非欧几里得空间进行3D感知预训练学习鲁棒且可泛化机器人操作策略的潜力。

Conclusion: 双曲空间为学习结构化嵌入提供了合适的几何特性，HyperMVP框架通过双曲多视图预训练显著提升了机器人操作任务的性能，证明了非欧几里得空间预训练在机器人应用中的价值。

Abstract: 3D-aware visual pretraining has proven effective in improving the performance of downstream robotic manipulation tasks. However, existing methods are constrained to Euclidean embedding spaces, whose flat geometry limits their ability to model structural relations among embeddings. As a result, they struggle to learn structured embeddings that are essential for robust spatial perception in robotic applications. To this end, we propose HyperMVP, a self-supervised framework for \underline{Hyper}bolic \underline{M}ulti\underline{V}iew \underline{P}retraining. Hyperbolic space offers geometric properties well suited for capturing structural relations. Methodologically, we extend the masked autoencoder paradigm and design a GeoLink encoder to learn multiview hyperbolic representations. The pretrained encoder is then finetuned with visuomotor policies on manipulation tasks. In addition, we introduce 3D-MOV, a large-scale dataset comprising multiple types of 3D point clouds to support pretraining. We evaluate HyperMVP on COLOSSEUM, RLBench, and real-world scenarios, where it consistently outperforms strong baselines across diverse tasks and perturbation settings. Our results highlight the potential of 3D-aware pretraining in a non-Euclidean space for learning robust and generalizable robotic manipulation policies.

</details>


### [205] [VPWEM: Non-Markovian Visuomotor Policy with Working and Episodic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04910)
*Yuheng Lei,Zhixuan Liang,Hongyuan Zhang,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VPWEM是一种具有工作记忆和情景记忆的非马尔可夫视觉运动策略，通过Transformer记忆压缩器将历史观察转换为固定数量的记忆标记，在保持计算效率的同时解决需要长期记忆的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略通常基于单步观察或短上下文历史，难以处理需要长期记忆的非马尔可夫任务。简单扩大上下文窗口会导致计算成本高、过拟合和实时性问题，而人类能够压缩重要经验为长期记忆来解决问题。

Method: 提出VPWEM：1) 保留最近观察标记的滑动窗口作为短期工作记忆；2) 引入基于Transformer的上下文记忆压缩器，递归地将窗口外观察转换为固定数量的情景记忆标记；3) 压缩器使用自注意力处理过去摘要标记缓存和交叉注意力处理历史观察缓存；4) 在扩散策略上实例化，联合训练策略和压缩器。

Result: 在MIKASA的记忆密集型操作任务上，VPWEM比包括扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作模型在内的最先进基线性能提升超过20%；在MoMaRT移动操作基准上平均提升5%。

Conclusion: VPWEM通过结合工作记忆和情景记忆，在保持每步近乎恒定内存和计算的同时，有效利用短期和整个情节的信息进行动作生成，显著提升了非马尔可夫任务的性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning from human demonstrations has achieved significant success in robotic control, yet most visuomotor policies still condition on single-step observations or short-context histories, making them struggle with non-Markovian tasks that require long-term memory. Simply enlarging the context window incurs substantial computational and memory costs and encourages overfitting to spurious correlations, leading to catastrophic failures under distribution shift and violating real-time constraints in robotic systems. By contrast, humans can compress important past experiences into long-term memories and exploit them to solve tasks throughout their lifetime. In this paper, we propose VPWEM, a non-Markovian visuomotor policy equipped with working and episodic memories. VPWEM retains a sliding window of recent observation tokens as short-term working memory, and introduces a Transformer-based contextual memory compressor that recursively converts out-of-window observations into a fixed number of episodic memory tokens. The compressor uses self-attention over a cache of past summary tokens and cross-attention over a cache of historical observations, and is trained jointly with the policy. We instantiate VPWEM on diffusion policies to exploit both short-term and episode-wide information for action generation with nearly constant memory and computation per step. Experiments demonstrate that VPWEM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines including diffusion policies and vision-language-action (VLA) models by more than 20% on the memory-intensive manipulation tasks in MIKASA and achieves an average 5% improvement on the mobile manipulation benchmark MoMaRT. Code is available at https://github.com/HarryLui98/code_vpwem.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond the Patch: Exploring Vulnerabilities of Visuomotor Policies via Viewpoint-Consistent 3D Adversarial Object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04913)
*Chanmi Lee,Minsung Yoon,Woojae Kim,Sebin Lee,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针对3D物体的视角一致对抗纹理优化方法，通过可微分渲染生成在动态视角下保持有效的对抗纹理，利用EOT和粗到细课程学习策略，结合显著性引导扰动和目标损失函数，在多种环境条件下有效攻击机器人视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D对抗补丁在固定摄像头设置下有效，但在动态视角（如腕戴式摄像头）下因透视畸变而效果下降。需要研究超越2D补丁的潜在漏洞，特别是在3D物体上生成视角一致的对抗纹理。

Method: 1. 通过可微分渲染实现3D物体的对抗纹理优化；2. 采用期望变换（EOT）和粗到细（C2F）课程学习策略；3. 利用距离依赖的频率特性生成在不同摄像头-物体距离下有效的纹理；4. 集成显著性引导扰动以重定向策略注意力；5. 设计目标损失函数持续驱动机器人朝向对抗物体。

Result: 该方法在多种环境条件下有效，证实了其黑盒可迁移性和实际应用可行性。对抗纹理在不同视角和距离下保持攻击效果，成功欺骗机器人视觉运动策略。

Conclusion: 提出的视角一致对抗纹理优化方法能够生成在动态视角下保持有效的3D对抗纹理，揭示了机器人视觉系统在3D场景中的新漏洞，为机器人安全研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Neural network-based visuomotor policies enable robots to perform manipulation tasks but remain susceptible to perceptual attacks. For example, conventional 2D adversarial patches are effective under fixed-camera setups, where appearance is relatively consistent; however, their efficacy often diminishes under dynamic viewpoints from moving cameras, such as wrist-mounted setups, due to perspective distortions. To proactively investigate potential vulnerabilities beyond 2D patches, this work proposes a viewpoint-consistent adversarial texture optimization method for 3D objects through differentiable rendering. As optimization strategies, we employ Expectation over Transformation (EOT) with a Coarse-to-Fine (C2F) curriculum, exploiting distance-dependent frequency characteristics to induce textures effective across varying camera-object distances. We further integrate saliency-guided perturbations to redirect policy attention and design a targeted loss that persistently drives robots toward adversarial objects. Our comprehensive experiments show that the proposed method is effective under various environmental conditions, while confirming its black-box transferability and real-world applicability.

</details>


### [207] [U-OBCA: Uncertainty-Aware Optimization-Based Collision Avoidance via Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Chance Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04914)
*Zehao Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Han Zhang,Jingchuan Wang,Weidong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: U-OBCA：一种基于优化的不确定性感知碰撞避免框架，通过概率机会约束处理多边形机器人与障碍物之间的碰撞风险，减少几何简化带来的保守性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性感知规划器通常使用简单几何基元（如圆形或椭圆）近似多边形机器人和障碍物，这显著缩小了可行空间，导致过于保守的轨迹，在狭窄环境中甚至可能导致规划失败。此外，许多方法依赖于特定的噪声分布假设，这些假设在实践中可能不成立，从而限制了其性能保证。

Method: 扩展基于优化的碰撞避免（OBCA）框架为不确定性感知的U-OBCA方法。该方法通过制定OBCA基的机会约束，显式考虑多边形机器人与障碍物之间的碰撞风险，避免几何简化。在温和的分布假设下，将这些概率约束收紧为确定性非线性约束，可通过标准数值优化求解器高效求解。

Result: 通过理论分析、数值仿真和真实世界实验验证了所提方法。结果表明，U-OBCA显著减轻了轨迹规划中的保守性，与现有基线方法相比实现了更高的导航效率，特别是在狭窄和杂乱的环境中。

Conclusion: U-OBCA框架通过处理多边形形状的不确定性感知碰撞避免，减少了不必要的保守性，提高了导航效率，特别是在狭窄和杂乱环境中表现优异，为机器人安全导航提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainties arising from localization error, trajectory prediction errors of the moving obstacles and environmental disturbances pose significant challenges to robot's safe navigation. Existing uncertainty-aware planners often approximate polygon-shaped robots and obstacles using simple geometric primitives such as circles or ellipses. Though computationally convenient, these approximations substantially shrink the feasible space, leading to overly conservative trajectories and even planning failure in narrow environments. In addition, many such methods rely on specific assumptions about noise distributions, which may not hold in practice and thus limit their performance guarantees. To address these limitations, we extend the Optimization-Based Collision Avoidance (OBCA) framework to an uncertainty-aware formulation, termed \emph{U-OBCA}. The proposed method explicitly accounts for the collision risk between polygon-shaped robots and obstacles by formulating OBCA-based chance constraints, and hence avoiding geometric simplifications and reducing unnecessary conservatism. These probabilistic constraints are further tightened into deterministic nonlinear constraints under mild distributional assumptions, which can be solved efficiently by standard numerical optimization solvers. The proposed approach is validated through theoretical analysis, numerical simulations and real-world experiments. The results demonstrate that U-OBCA significantly mitigates the conservatism in trajectory planning and achieves higher navigation efficiency compared to existing baseline methods, particularly in narrow and cluttered environments.

</details>


### [208] [Integrated cooperative localization of heterogeneous measurement swarm: A unified data-driven method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04932)
*Kunrui Ze,Wei Wang,Guibin Sun,Jiaqi Yan,Kexin Liu,Jinhu Lü*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于异构机器人系统的协同定位方法，通过数据驱动的自适应相对定位估计器和分布式位姿耦合策略，在弱连通有向测量拓扑下实现协同定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人系统具有不同的测量能力，导致有向稀疏的测量拓扑，而现有协同定位方法大多依赖多边定位和严格的多邻居几何要求，难以适应这种异构性。

Method: 1) 开发统一的数据驱动自适应相对定位估计器，处理异构和单向测量；2) 基于收敛的相对定位估计，设计分布式位姿耦合协同定位策略，在弱连通有向测量拓扑下工作。

Result: 该方法在弱连通有向测量拓扑下保证协同定位，这是现有结果中最不严格的条件。通过编队控制应用和真实世界实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法不依赖特定控制任务，能够有效解决异构机器人系统的协同定位问题，特别适用于具有不同测量能力和有向稀疏测量拓扑的实际场景。

Abstract: The cooperative localization (CL) problem in heterogeneous robotic systems with different measurement capabilities is investigated in this work. In practice, heterogeneous sensors lead to directed and sparse measurement topologies, whereas most existing CL approaches rely on multilateral localization with restrictive multi-neighbor geometric requirements. To overcome this limitation, we enable pairwise relative localization (RL) between neighboring robots using only mutual measurement and odometry information. A unified data-driven adaptive RL estimator is first developed to handle heterogeneous and unidirectional measurements. Based on the convergent RL estimates, a distributed pose-coupling CL strategy is then designed, which guarantees CL under a weakly connected directed measurement topology, representing the least restrictive condition among existing results. The proposed method is independent of specific control tasks and is validated through a formation control application and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [209] [Observer Design for Augmented Reality-based Teleoperation of Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05015)
*Jorge Francisco García-Samartín,Iago López Pérez,Emirhan Yolcu,Jaime del Cerro,Antonio Barrientos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种用于软体机器人遥操作的增强现实界面，通过Microsoft HoloLens 2和中央计算机实现，在气动软体机械臂PETER上验证，位置估计误差约为机器人长度的5%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然虚拟现实和增强现实已广泛用于刚性机器人遥操作，但由于软体机器人建模困难，缺乏准确且计算高效的表示方法，尚未应用于软体机器人领域

Method: 开发基于Microsoft HoloLens 2增强现实眼镜和中央计算机的系统，利用传感器数据，基于虚拟现实程序的物理引擎估计软体机器人位置，实现增强现实遥操作界面

Result: 在高度模块化气动软体机械臂PETER上进行验证，获得的位置估计误差约为机器人长度的5%，证明增强现实能够促进操作员与软体机械臂的交互，并可集成到控制回路中

Conclusion: 增强现实界面成功实现了软体机器人的遥操作，解决了软体机器人建模困难的问题，为软体机器人的人机交互提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Although virtual and augmented reality are gaining traction as teleoperation tools for various types of robots, including manipulators and mobile robots, they are not being used for soft robots. The inherent difficulties of modelling soft robots mean that combining accurate and computationally efficient representations is very challenging. This paper presents an augmented reality interface for teleoperating these devices. The developed system consists of Microsoft HoloLens 2 glasses and a central computer responsible for calculations. Validation is performed on PETER, a highly modular pneumatic manipulator. Using data collected from sensors, the computer estimates the robot's position based on the physics of the virtual reality programme. Errors obtained are on the order of 5% of the robot's length, demonstrating that augmented reality facilitates operator interaction with soft manipulators and can be integrated into the control loop.

</details>


### [210] [Direct Contact-Tolerant Motion Planning With Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05017)
*He Li,Jian Sun,Chengyang Li,Guoliang Li,Qiyu Ruan,Shuai Wang,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出直接接触容忍规划器，通过视觉语言模型实现点云分割和导航，在杂乱环境中实现鲁棒高效导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触容忍运动规划方法依赖间接空间表示（如预建地图、障碍物集合），导致不准确且缺乏对环境不确定性的适应性，需要更直接的方法

Method: 提出直接接触容忍规划器，包含两个关键组件：1）VLM点云分割器，在图像空间进行接触容忍推理，生成接触感知点云；2）VPP引导导航，将CTMP公式化为感知到控制的优化问题，通过专门DNN求解

Result: 在Isaac Sim和真实汽车式机器人上实现，在具有可移动障碍物的杂乱环境中实现鲁棒高效导航，在多项指标上优于代表性基线方法

Conclusion: 直接接触容忍规划器通过集成视觉语言模型到直接点感知和导航中，解决了现有CTMP方法的局限性，实现了对杂乱环境中可移动障碍物的自适应导航

Abstract: Navigation in cluttered environments often requires robots to tolerate contact with movable or deformable objects to maintain efficiency. Existing contact-tolerant motion planning (CTMP) methods rely on indirect spatial representations (e.g., prebuilt map, obstacle set), resulting in inaccuracies and a lack of adaptiveness to environmental uncertainties. To address this issue, we propose a direct contact-tolerant (DCT) planner, which integrates vision-language models (VLMs) into direct point perception and navigation, including two key components. The first one is VLM point cloud partitioner (VPP), which performs contact-tolerance reasoning in image space using VLM, caches inference masks, propagates them across frames using odometry, and projects them onto the current scan to generate a contact-aware point cloud. The second innovation is VPP guided navigation (VGN), which formulates CTMP as a perception-to-control optimization problem under direct contact-aware point cloud constraints, which is further solved by a specialized deep neural network (DNN). We implement DCT in Isaac Sim and a real car-like robot, demonstrating that DCT achieves robust and efficient navigation in cluttered environments with movable obstacles, outperforming representative baselines across diverse metrics. The code is available at: https://github.com/ChrisLeeUM/DCT.

</details>


### [211] [VinePT-Map: Pole-Trunk Semantic Mapping for Resilient Autonomous Robotics in Vineyards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05070)
*Giorgio Audrito,Mauro Martini,Alessandro Navone,Giorgia Galluzzo,Marcello Chiaberge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VinePT-Map：基于葡萄树干和支撑杆作为持久结构地标的语义建图框架，用于实现季节无关的农业机器人定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业环境中自主机器人的长期可靠部署面临挑战，包括感知混淆、季节变化和作物冠层动态变化。葡萄园具有重复的行结构和显著的物候阶段视觉变化，限制了传统基于特征的定位和建图方法的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出VinePT-Map语义建图框架，将葡萄树干和支撑杆作为持久结构地标。采用因子图建模建图问题，集成GPS、IMU和RGB-D观测，通过利用葡萄园结构的鲁棒几何约束。基于实例分割和跟踪的高效感知流水线，结合聚类滤波器进行离群值剔除和位姿优化，使用低成本传感器和板载计算实现准确的地标检测。

Result: 提出了用于树干和杆分割跟踪的多季节数据集。跨多个季节的广泛实地实验证明了该方法的鲁棒性和准确性，突出了其在农业环境中长期自主操作的适用性。

Conclusion: VinePT-Map框架通过利用葡萄园中的持久结构地标，实现了季节无关的机器人定位，为农业环境中长期可靠的自主操作提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable long-term deployment of autonomous robots in agricultural environments remains challenging due to perceptual aliasing, seasonal variability, and the dynamic nature of crop canopies. Vineyards, characterized by repetitive row structures and significant visual changes across phenological stages, represent a pivotal field challenge, limiting the robustness of conventional feature-based localization and mapping approaches. This paper introduces VinePT-Map, a semantic mapping framework that leverages vine trunks and support poles as persistent structural landmarks to enable season-agnostic and resilient robot localization. The proposed method formulates the mapping problem as a factor graph, integrating GPS, IMU, and RGB-D observations through robust geometrical constraints that exploit vineyard structure. An efficient perception pipeline based on instance segmentation and tracking, combined with a clustering filter for outlier rejection and pose refinement, enables accurate landmark detection using low-cost sensors and onboard computation. To validate the pipeline, we present a multi-season dataset for trunk and pole segmentation and tracking. Extensive field experiments conducted across diverse seasons demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of the proposed approach, highlighting its suitability for long-term autonomous operation in agricultural environments.

</details>


### [212] [AIM-SLAM: Dense Monocular SLAM via Adaptive and Informative Multi-View Keyframe Prioritization with Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05097)
*Jinwoo Jeon,Dong-Uk Seo,Eungchang Mason Lee,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AIM-SLAM：一种利用自适应信息多视图关键帧优先级的密集单目SLAM框架，通过VGGT的密集点云预测和SIGMA模块实现高效视图选择，在姿态估计和密集重建方面达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型在单目SLAM中仍局限于两视图对或固定长度输入，缺乏对几何上下文视图选择的充分考量，需要更智能的多视图选择机制来提升密集重建性能。

Method: 提出AIM-SLAM框架，包含：1）SIGMA模块，利用体素重叠和信息增益检索候选关键帧集并自适应确定其大小；2）联合多视图Sim(3)优化，强制选定视图间的一致性对齐；3）基于VGGT的密集点云预测。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证，AIM-SLAM在姿态估计和密集重建方面均达到最先进性能，支持ROS集成，代码已开源。

Conclusion: AIM-SLAM通过自适应信息多视图关键帧优先级和几何感知的视图选择机制，有效解决了现有几何基础模型在密集单目SLAM中的局限性，显著提升了重建精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent advances in geometric foundation models have emerged as a promising alternative for addressing the challenge of dense reconstruction in monocular visual simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). Although geometric foundation models enable SLAM to leverage variable input views, the previous methods remain confined to two-view pairs or fixed-length inputs without sufficient deliberation of geometric context for view selection. To tackle this problem, we propose AIM-SLAM, a dense monocular SLAM framework that exploits an adaptive and informative multi-view keyframe prioritization with dense pointmap predictions from visual geometry grounded transformer (VGGT). Specifically, we introduce the selective information- and geometric-aware multi-view adaptation (SIGMA) module, which employs voxel overlap and information gain to retrieve a candidate set of keyframes and adaptively determine its size. Furthermore, we formulate a joint multi-view Sim(3) optimization that enforces consistent alignment across selected views, substantially improving pose estimation accuracy. The effectiveness of AIM-SLAM is demonstrated on real-world datasets, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance in both pose estimation and dense reconstruction. Our system supports ROS integration, with code is available at https://aimslam.github.io/.

</details>


### [213] [GaussTwin: Unified Simulation and Correction with Gaussian Splatting for Robotic Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05108)
*Yichen Cai,Paul Jansonnie,Cristiana de Farias,Oleg Arenz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussTwin：一种实时数字孪生系统，结合基于位置的动力学、离散Cosserat杆公式和Gaussian splatting，用于物理基础的机器人操作模拟和视觉校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生系统缺乏统一模型、难以处理复杂动态交互、存在真实-模拟差距，限制了模型预测控制等下游应用。

Method: 结合基于位置的动力学和离散Cosserat杆公式进行物理基础模拟，使用Gaussian splatting进行高效渲染和视觉校正。通过将高斯锚定到物理基元，并强制执行由光度误差和分割掩码驱动的连贯SE(3)更新。

Result: 在仿真和Franka Research 3平台上实验表明，GaussTwin相比形状匹配和纯刚性基线，在跟踪精度和鲁棒性方面持续改进，同时支持推式规划等下游任务。

Conclusion: GaussTwin是迈向统一、物理意义明确的数字孪生系统的一步，能够支持闭环机器人交互和学习。

Abstract: Digital twins promise to enhance robotic manipulation by maintaining a consistent link between real-world perception and simulation. However, most existing systems struggle with the lack of a unified model, complex dynamic interactions, and the real-to-sim gap, which limits downstream applications such as model predictive control. Thus, we propose GaussTwin, a real-time digital twin that combines position-based dynamics with discrete Cosserat rod formulations for physically grounded simulation, and Gaussian splatting for efficient rendering and visual correction. By anchoring Gaussians to physical primitives and enforcing coherent SE(3) updates driven by photometric error and segmentation masks, GaussTwin achieves stable prediction-correction while preserving physical fidelity. Through experiments in both simulation and on a Franka Research 3 platform, we show that GaussTwin consistently improves tracking accuracy and robustness compared to shape-matching and rigid-only baselines, while also enabling downstream tasks such as push-based planning. These results highlight GaussTwin as a step toward unified, physically meaningful digital twins that can support closed-loop robotic interaction and learning.

</details>


### [214] [SPIRIT: Perceptive Shared Autonomy for Robust Robotic Manipulation under Deep Learning Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05111)
*Jongseok Lee,Ribin Balachandran,Harsimran Singh,Jianxiang Feng,Hrishik Mishra,Marco De Stefano,Rudolph Triebel,Alin Albu-Schaeffer,Konstantin Kondak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"感知共享自主性"概念，利用深度学习感知的不确定性估计来调节自主性水平：感知置信时启用半自主操作提升性能，不确定性增加时转为触觉遥操作保持鲁棒性，从而安全整合高性能但不可解释的DL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在机器人感知方面取得显著进展，但其有限的鲁棒性和缺乏可解释性阻碍了在安全关键应用中的可靠部署。需要一种方法将高性能但不可解释的DL方法安全整合到机器人系统中。

Method: 提出感知共享自主性概念，基于神经正切核(NTK)开发不确定性感知的深度学习点云配准方法。系统根据感知置信度动态调节自主性水平：高置信时启用半自主操作，不确定性增加时转为触觉遥操作。

Result: 通过15名参与者的用户研究和模拟工业场景评估，系统在DL感知失败时仍能实现可靠的机器人操作。SPIRIT系统同时提升了操作性能和系统可靠性，并入选主要工业创新奖决赛。

Conclusion: 感知共享自主性概念通过不确定性感知调节自主性水平，成功将高性能但不可解释的DL方法安全整合到机器人系统中，在保持鲁棒性的同时提升了操作性能。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) has enabled impressive advances in robotic perception, yet its limited robustness and lack of interpretability hinder reliable deployment in safety critical applications. We propose a concept termed perceptive shared autonomy, in which uncertainty estimates from DL based perception are used to regulate the level of autonomy. Specifically, when the robot's perception is confident, semi-autonomous manipulation is enabled to improve performance; when uncertainty increases, control transitions to haptic teleoperation for maintaining robustness. In this way, high-performing but uninterpretable DL methods can be integrated safely into robotic systems. A key technical enabler is an uncertainty aware DL based point cloud registration approach based on the so called Neural Tangent Kernels (NTK). We evaluate perceptive shared autonomy on challenging aerial manipulation tasks through a user study of 15 participants and realization of mock-up industrial scenarios, demonstrating reliable robotic manipulation despite failures in DL based perception. The resulting system, named SPIRIT, improves both manipulation performance and system reliability. SPIRIT was selected as a finalist of a major industrial innovation award.

</details>


### [215] [Critic in the Loop: A Tri-System VLA Framework for Robust Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05185)
*Pengfei Yi,Yingjie Ma,Wenjiang Xu,Yanan Hao,Shuai Gan,Wanting Li,Shanlin Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Critic in the Loop框架，通过动态VLM-专家调度平衡高级语义推理与低级反应控制，解决视觉机器人操作中认知规划与实时执行的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉机器人操作面临的核心挑战：视觉语言模型(VLMs)擅长认知规划但推理延迟高，无法实时执行；而快速视觉语言动作(VLA)模型缺乏复杂长时程任务所需的语义深度。需要桥接这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出仿生三系统架构：VLM大脑负责全局推理，VLA小脑负责反应执行，轻量级视觉Critic持续监控工作空间并动态路由控制权。Critic通过VLA维持快速闭环执行常规子任务，检测到执行异常时自适应触发VLM重新规划。集成类人规则打破无限重试循环。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长时程操作基准测试中达到最先进性能。视觉基础调度最小化昂贵的VLM查询，同时显著增强系统在分布外(OOD)场景下的鲁棒性和自主性。

Conclusion: Critic in the Loop框架通过动态VLM-专家调度有效平衡了高级语义推理与低级反应控制，解决了视觉机器人操作中的核心挑战，实现了高性能、鲁棒且自主的长时程操作。

Abstract: Balancing high-level semantic reasoning with low-level reactive control remains a core challenge in visual robotic manipulation. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at cognitive planning, their inference latency precludes real-time execution. Conversely, fast Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often lack the semantic depth required for complex, long-horizon tasks. To bridge this gap, we introduce Critic in the Loop, an adaptive hierarchical framework driven by dynamic VLM-Expert scheduling. At its core is a bionic Tri-System architecture comprising a VLM brain for global reasoning, a VLA cerebellum for reactive execution, and a lightweight visual Critic. By continuously monitoring the workspace, the Critic dynamically routes control authority. It sustains rapid closed-loop execution via the VLA for routine subtasks, and adaptively triggers the VLM for replanning upon detecting execution anomalies such as task stagnation or failures. Furthermore, our architecture seamlessly integrates human-inspired rules to intuitively break infinite retry loops. This visually-grounded scheduling minimizes expensive VLM queries, while substantially enhancing system robustness and autonomy in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Comprehensive experiments on challenging, long-horizon manipulation benchmarks reveal that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [216] [Rethinking the Role of Collaborative Robots in Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05252)
*Vivek Gupte,Shalutha Rajapakshe,Emmanuel Senft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出扩展协作机器人在物理康复中的应用范围，不仅关注重复运动训练，还设想在治疗前、中、后阶段协助治疗师和患者，以改善治疗可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前协作机器人在物理康复中的研究主要集中于患者的重复运动训练，忽略了治疗过程中其他可能受益于机器人协作的阶段。同时，残疾人和慢性病患者获得物理治疗的机会有限。协作机器人有潜力支持患者和治疗师，并改善治疗可及性，但其更广泛的潜力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出概念性框架，将协作机器人的作用范围扩展到治疗前、治疗中和治疗后三个阶段。讨论协作机器人如何通过促进基于能力的治疗设计、帮助治疗师管理时间和精力来消除可及性障碍。识别实现这些角色面临的挑战。

Result: 提出了协作机器人在物理康复中扩展作用范围的新视角，识别了三个关键挑战：推进用户状态理解、确保安全性、将协作机器人整合到治疗师工作流程中。这一视角为HRI社区在辅助机器人领域的进展提供了新的研究问题和机会。

Conclusion: 协作机器人在物理康复中具有超越当前重复运动训练应用的更广泛潜力。通过设想在治疗前、中、后阶段协助治疗师和患者，协作机器人可以改善治疗可及性并支持治疗师工作。实现这一愿景需要解决用户状态理解、安全性和工作流程整合等关键挑战，这为HRI研究社区开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Current research on collaborative robots (cobots) in physical rehabilitation largely focuses on repeated motion training for people undergoing physical therapy (PuPT), even though these sessions include phases that could benefit from robotic collaboration and assistance. Meanwhile, access to physical therapy remains limited for people with disabilities and chronic illnesses. Cobots could support both PuPT and therapists, and improve access to therapy, yet their broader potential remains underexplored. We propose extending the scope of cobots by imagining their role in assisting therapists and PuPT before, during, and after a therapy session. We discuss how cobot assistance may lift access barriers by promoting ability-based therapy design and helping therapists manage their time and effort. Finally, we highlight challenges to realizing these roles, including advancing user-state understanding, ensuring safety, and integrating cobots into therapists' workflow. This view opens new research questions and opportunities to draw from the HRI community's advances in assistive robotics.

</details>


### [217] [From Code to Road: A Vehicle-in-the-Loop and Digital Twin-Based Framework for Central Car Server Testing in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05279)
*Chengdong Wu,Sven Kirchner,Nils Purschke,Axel Torschmied,Norbert Kroth,Yinglei Song,André Schamschurko,Erik Leo Haß,Kuo-Yi Chao,Yi Zhang,Nenad Petrovic,Alois C. Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于车辆在环和数字孪生技术的汽车软件测试框架，将物理测试车辆与虚拟仿真环境耦合，为集中式E/E架构提供安全、可重复、真实且经济高效的自动驾驶算法验证平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯虚拟仿真难以准确捕捉所有现实因素，而传统车辆在环方法存在硬件集成需求高、测试效率低等问题。需要一种能够准确反映集中式E/E架构行为、减少开发集成工作量的测试方法。

Method: 开发基于车辆在环和数字孪生技术的测试框架，将测功机上的物理测试车辆与其同步的虚拟对应物耦合。框架在仿真过程后直接在车辆硬件上运行完整的自动驾驶软件，无需刷写和中间层，实现无缝的虚拟-物理集成。

Result: 实验案例研究表明该框架在不同测试场景中具有有效性。与传统方法相比，消除了单独测试物理ECU及其通信协议的需求，减少了早期开发阶段的全硬件集成需求。

Conclusion: 该框架为集中式E/E架构的自动驾驶算法验证提供了安全、可重复、真实且经济高效的平台，具有减少未来自动驾驶流水线开发和集成工作量的潜力。

Abstract: Simulation is one of the most essential parts in the development stage of automotive software. However, purely virtual simulations often struggle to accurately capture all real-world factors due to limitations in modeling. To address this challenge, this work presents a test framework for automotive software on the centralized E/E architecture, which is a central car server in our case, based on Vehicle-in-the-Loop (ViL) and digital twin technology. The framework couples a physical test vehicle on a dynamometer test bench with its synchronized virtual counterpart in a simulation environment. Our approach provides a safe, reproducible, realistic, and cost-effective platform for validating autonomous driving algorithms with a centralized architecture. This test method eliminates the need to test individual physical ECUs and their communication protocols separately. In contrast to traditional ViL methods, the proposed framework runs the full autonomous driving software directly on the vehicle hardware after the simulation process, eliminating flashing and intermediate layers while enabling seamless virtual-physical integration and accurately reflecting centralized E/E behavior. In addition, incorporating mixed testing in both simulated and physical environments reduces the need for full hardware integration during the early stages of automotive development. Experimental case studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework in different test scenarios. These findings highlight the potential to reduce development and integration efforts for testing autonomous driving pipelines in the future.

</details>


### [218] [Constraint-Free Static Modeling of Continuum Parallel Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05309)
*Lingxiao Xun,Matyas Diezinger,Azad Artinian,Guillaume Laurent,Brahim Tamadazte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何精确、基于配置且无约束的连续体并联机器人静态模型，通过运动学嵌入消除连接约束，使用SE(3)上的节点位姿离散化杆件，采用牛顿迭代求解非线性平衡方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体并联机器人结合刚性驱动机构和多个弹性杆件，当刚性-连续体连接点通过显式运动学约束强制时，正向静力学分析变得困难。基于约束的公式通常引入额外的代数变量，使数值求解和后续控制复杂化。

Method: 提出几何精确、基于配置且无约束的静态模型：1) 通过运动学嵌入消除连接约束；2) 在SE(3)上使用节点位姿离散化每个杆件；3) 通过线性应变参数化重建单元应变场；4) 使用四阶Magnus近似建立单元端部位姿与应变之间的显式几何一致映射；5) 基于总势能和虚功推导装配就绪的残差和显式牛顿切线；6) 在乘积流形上使用黎曼牛顿迭代求解非线性平衡方程。

Result: 在三伺服电机、六杆原型上的实验验证了模型的有效性，仿真结果与测量结果在无负载运动和外部加载情况下均显示出良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功消除了传统约束公式的复杂性，提供了一种几何精确、无约束的连续体并联机器人静态建模框架，在几何非线性、大变形和大旋转条件下保持有效，为后续控制应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Continuum parallel robots (CPR) combine rigid actuation mechanisms with multiple elastic rods in a closed-loop topology, making forward statics challenging when rigid--continuum junctions are enforced by explicit kinematic constraints. Such constraint-based formulations typically introduce additional algebraic variables and complicate both numerical solution and downstream control. This paper presents a geometric exact, configuration-based and constraint-free static model of CPR that remains valid under geometrically nonlinear, large-deformation and large-rotation conditions. Connectivity constraints are eliminated by kinematic embedding, yielding a reduced unconstrained problem. Each rod of CPR is discretized by nodal poses on SE(3), while the element-wise strain field is reconstructed through a linear strain parameterization. A fourth-order Magnus approximation yields an explicit and geometrically consistent mapping between element end poses and the strain. Rigid attachments at the motor-driven base and the end-effector platforms are handled through kinematic embeddings. Based on total potential energy and virtual work, we derive assembly-ready residuals and explicit Newton tangents, and solve the resulting nonlinear equilibrium equations using a Riemannian Newton iteration on the product manifold. Experiments on a three-servomotor, six-rod prototype validate the model by showing good agreement between simulation and measurements for both unloaded motions and externally loaded cases.

</details>


### [219] [UltraDexGrasp: Learning Universal Dexterous Grasping for Bimanual Robots with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05312)
*Sizhe Yang,Yiman Xie,Zhixuan Liang,Yang Tian,Jia Zeng,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UltraDexGrasp框架，用于双手机器人的通用灵巧抓取，通过优化和规划生成高质量数据，构建2000万帧数据集，训练出能实现零样本仿真到现实迁移的抓取策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能根据物体特性自主选择抓取策略，而当前机器人抓取在多策略设置中仍受限，特别是双手机器人的灵巧抓取研究不足，数据是主要瓶颈，需要实现物理合理、几何贴合且能抵抗外力的抓取

Method: 提出UltraDexGrasp框架，包含数据生成管道（集成基于优化的抓取合成和基于规划的演示生成），构建UltraDexGrasp-20M数据集（2000万帧，1000个物体），开发基于点云输入、通过单向注意力聚合场景特征、预测控制指令的抓取策略

Result: 仅使用合成数据训练的策略实现了鲁棒的零样本仿真到现实迁移，在真实世界通用灵巧抓取中平均成功率达81.2%，能处理不同形状、尺寸和重量的新物体

Conclusion: UltraDexGrasp框架有效解决了双手机器人灵巧抓取的数据瓶颈问题，通过大规模合成数据训练的策略实现了优秀的零样本迁移性能，为双手机器人抓取研究提供了开源数据生成管道

Abstract: Grasping is a fundamental capability for robots to interact with the physical world. Humans, equipped with two hands, autonomously select appropriate grasp strategies based on the shape, size, and weight of objects, enabling robust grasping and subsequent manipulation. In contrast, current robotic grasping remains limited, particularly in multi-strategy settings. Although substantial efforts have targeted parallel-gripper and single-hand grasping, dexterous grasping for bimanual robots remains underexplored, with data being a primary bottleneck. Achieving physically plausible and geometrically conforming grasps that can withstand external wrenches poses significant challenges. To address these issues, we introduce UltraDexGrasp, a framework for universal dexterous grasping with bimanual robots. The proposed data-generation pipeline integrates optimization-based grasp synthesis with planning-based demonstration generation, yielding high-quality and diverse trajectories across multiple grasp strategies. With this framework, we curate UltraDexGrasp-20M, a large-scale, multi-strategy grasp dataset comprising 20 million frames across 1,000 objects. Based on UltraDexGrasp-20M, we further develop a simple yet effective grasp policy that takes point clouds as input, aggregates scene features via unidirectional attention, and predicts control commands. Trained exclusively on synthetic data, the policy achieves robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and consistently succeeds on novel objects with varied shapes, sizes, and weights, attaining an average success rate of 81.2% in real-world universal dexterous grasping. To facilitate future research on grasping with bimanual robots, we open-source the data generation pipeline at https://github.com/InternRobotics/UltraDexGrasp.

</details>


### [220] [CT-Enabled Patient-Specific Simulation and Contact-Aware Robotic Planning for Cochlear Implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05333)
*Lingxiao Xun,Gang Zheng,Alexandre Kruszewski,Renato Torres*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一CT到仿真流程，用于机器人辅助人工耳蜗植入的接触感知规划与验证，通过微分Cosserat杆模型和患者特异性解剖重建，实现接触力预测与调控，降低创伤风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助人工耳蜗植入需要精确预测和调控接触力，以最小化耳蜗内创伤并防止锁定和屈曲等失效模式。需要整合先进医学影像与机器人技术，实现自主精准干预。

Method: 开发低维微分Cosserat杆模型，结合摩擦接触和伪动力学正则化；从CT影像重建患者特异性耳蜗解剖，通过解析参数化编码鼓阶腔；基于微分平衡约束公式，推导在线方向更新律；通过仿真和台架实验验证。

Result: 仿真和实验验证了变形和力趋势，证明能够降低锁定/屈曲风险并提高插入深度。CT影像增强了机器人辅助内耳手术的建模、规划和安全性能力。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了基于CT的统一仿真流程如何提升机器人辅助人工耳蜗植入的安全性，通过接触感知规划和患者特异性建模，为精准医疗干预提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Robotic cochlear-implant (CI) insertion requires precise prediction and regulation of contact forces to minimize intracochlear trauma and prevent failure modes such as locking and buckling. Aligned with the integration of advanced medical imaging and robotics for autonomous, precision interventions, this paper presents a unified CT-to-simulation pipeline for contact-aware insertion planning and validation. We develop a low-dimensional, differentiable Cosserat-rod model of the electrode array coupled with frictional contact and pseudo-dynamics regularization to ensure continuous stick-slip transitions. Patient-specific cochlear anatomy is reconstructed from CT imaging and encoded via an analytic parametrization of the scala-tympani lumen, enabling efficient and differentiable contact queries through closest-point projection. Based on a differentiated equilibrium-constraint formulation, we derive an online direction-update law under an RCM-like constraint that suppresses lateral insertion forces while maintaining axial advancement. Simulations and benchtop experiments validate deformation and force trends, demonstrating reduced locking/buckling risk and improved insertion depth. The study highlights how CT-based imaging enhances modeling, planning, and safety capabilities in robot-assisted inner-ear procedures.

</details>


### [221] [Omni-Manip: Beyond-FOV Large-Workspace Humanoid Manipulation with Omnidirectional 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05355)
*Pei Qu,Zheng Li,Yufei Jia,Ziyun Liu,Liang Zhu,Haoang Li,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Omni-Manip：一种基于LiDAR的端到端3D视觉运动策略，通过全景点云处理实现大工作空间中的稳健操作，无需频繁重新定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在非结构化环境中进行灵巧操作面临感知限制的挑战。当物理约束阻止机器人重新定位时，全向感知比颜色或语义信息更为关键。传统RGB-D解决方案存在视野狭窄和自遮挡问题，需要频繁移动底座，引入运动不确定性和安全风险。现有扩展感知的方法（如主动视觉系统和第三方视角相机）存在机械复杂性、校准依赖性和延迟问题，影响实时性能。

Method: 提出Omni-Manip，一种端到端的LiDAR驱动3D视觉运动策略。方法处理全景点云，通过时间感知注意力池化机制高效编码稀疏3D数据并捕捉时间依赖性。360°感知使机器人能在广阔区域内与物体交互而无需频繁重新定位。为支持策略学习，开发了全身遥操作系统用于高效收集全身协调数据。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的广泛实验表明，Omni-Manip在大工作空间和杂乱场景中实现了稳健性能，优于依赖自我中心深度相机的基线方法。

Conclusion: Omni-Manip通过LiDAR驱动的全景感知解决了人形机器人在受限环境中的操作挑战，提供了一种无需频繁重新定位的稳健解决方案，在保持实时性能的同时扩展了有效工作空间。

Abstract: The deployment of humanoid robots for dexterous manipulation in unstructured environments remains challenging due to perceptual limitations that constrain the effective workspace. In scenarios where physical constraints prevent the robot from repositioning itself, maintaining omnidirectional awareness becomes far more critical than color or semantic information. While recent advances in visuomotor policy learning have improved manipulation capabilities, conventional RGB-D solutions suffer from narrow fields of view (FOV) and self-occlusion, requiring frequent base movements that introduce motion uncertainty and safety risks. Existing approaches to expanding perception, including active vision systems and third-view cameras, introduce mechanical complexity, calibration dependencies, and latency that hinder reliable real-time performance. In this work, We propose Omni-Manip, an end-to-end LiDAR-driven 3D visuomotor policy that enables robust manipulation in large workspaces. Our method processes panoramic point clouds through a Time-Aware Attention Pooling mechanism, efficiently encoding sparse 3D data while capturing temporal dependencies. This 360° perception allows the robot to interact with objects across wide areas without frequent repositioning. To support policy learning, we develop a whole-body teleoperation system for efficient data collection on full-body coordination. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world environments show that Omni-Manip achieves robust performance in large-workspace and cluttered scenarios, outperforming baselines that rely on egocentric depth cameras.

</details>


### [222] [OpenFrontier: General Navigation with Visual-Language Grounded Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05377)
*Esteban Padilla,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenFrontier：一种无需训练、基于视觉-语言先验模型的零样本导航框架，通过将导航问题转化为稀疏子目标识别与到达问题，使用语义锚点实现高效目标条件导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法依赖密集3D重建和手工设计的目标度量，泛化能力有限；现有视觉-语言导航模型需要交互训练、大规模数据收集或特定任务微调。需要一种无需训练、能灵活适应不同任务和环境的导航框架。

Method: 将导航问题重新定义为稀疏子目标识别与到达问题，选择导航前沿作为语义锚点，构建OpenFrontier框架：无需密集3D建图、策略训练或模型微调，仅集成现有视觉-语言先验模型，通过语义锚点提供高层语义先验的视觉锚定目标。

Result: 在多个导航基准测试中展现出强大的零样本性能，并在移动机器人上实现了有效的现实世界部署，验证了框架的实用性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将导航问题重新定义为稀疏子目标识别与到达问题，并利用语义锚点整合视觉-语言先验模型，OpenFrontier实现了无需训练的高效目标条件导航，为开放世界导航提供了轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Open-world navigation requires robots to make decisions in complex everyday environments while adapting to flexible task requirements. Conventional navigation approaches often rely on dense 3D reconstruction and hand-crafted goal metrics, which limits their generalization across tasks and environments. Recent advances in vision--language navigation (VLN) and vision--language--action (VLA) models enable end-to-end policies conditioned on natural language, but typically require interactive training, large-scale data collection, or task-specific fine-tuning with a mobile agent. We formulate navigation as a sparse subgoal identification and reaching problem and observe that providing visual anchoring targets for high-level semantic priors enables highly efficient goal-conditioned navigation. Based on this insight, we select navigation frontiers as semantic anchors and propose OpenFrontier, a training-free navigation framework that seamlessly integrates diverse vision--language prior models. OpenFrontier enables efficient navigation with a lightweight system design, without dense 3D mapping, policy training, or model fine-tuning. We evaluate OpenFrontier across multiple navigation benchmarks and demonstrate strong zero-shot performance, as well as effective real-world deployment on a mobile robot.

</details>


### [223] [Accelerating Sampling-Based Control via Learned Linear Koopman Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05385)
*Wenjian Hao,Yuxuan Fang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MPPI-DK控制框架，用学习的深度Koopman算子模型替代非线性动力学，在保持控制性能的同时显著提升计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MPPI控制框架在处理复杂非线性动力学系统时计算成本高，需要提高计算效率同时保持控制性能

Method: 用学习的线性深度Koopman算子模型替代非线性动力学进行轨迹传播，直接从交互数据学习动力学模型，无需解析系统模型

Result: 在摆平衡和表面车辆导航仿真任务中评估，并在四足机器人硬件上验证；MPPI-DK在保持接近真实动力学MPPI控制性能的同时，显著降低计算成本

Conclusion: MPPI-DK框架能够实现高效的实时控制，在机器人平台上显著降低计算负担同时保持良好控制性能

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient model predictive path integral (MPPI) control framework for systems with complex nonlinear dynamics. To improve the computational efficiency of classic MPPI while preserving control performance, we replace the nonlinear dynamics used for trajectory propagation with a learned linear deep Koopman operator (DKO) model, enabling faster rollout and more efficient trajectory sampling. The DKO dynamics are learned directly from interaction data, eliminating the need for analytical system models. The resulting controller, termed MPPI-DK, is evaluated in simulation on pendulum balancing and surface vehicle navigation tasks, and validated on hardware through reference-tracking experiments on a quadruped robot. Experimental results demonstrate that MPPI-DK achieves control performance close to MPPI with true dynamics while substantially reducing computational cost, enabling efficient real-time control on robotic platforms.

</details>


### [224] [Loop Closure via Maximal Cliques in 3D LiDAR-Based SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05397)
*Javier Laserna,Saurabh Gupta,Oscar Martinez Mozos,Cyrill Stachniss,Pablo San Segundo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CliReg：一种用于3D LiDAR SLAM中闭环验证的确定性算法，通过最大团搜索替代RANSAC，提高在噪声和异常值情况下的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D LiDAR SLAM中的闭环检测在传感器噪声、环境模糊性和视角变化条件下面临挑战。RANSAC方法在存在异常值时可能失败，导致地图不一致。需要更鲁棒的闭环验证方法。

Method: 提出CliReg算法，用特征对应兼容性图的最大团搜索替代RANSAC验证。集成到实时流水线中，使用二进制3D描述符和基于汉明距离嵌入的二叉搜索树匹配。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的评估显示，相比RANSAC，CliReg在稀疏或模糊条件下始终获得更低的位姿误差和更可靠的闭环。在2D投影地图上的实验也验证了其跨空间域的通用性。

Conclusion: CliReg为闭环检测提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案，通过确定性最大团搜索避免了RANSAC的随机采样问题，在噪声和异常值存在时表现更可靠。

Abstract: Reliable loop closure detection remains a critical challenge in 3D LiDAR-based SLAM, especially under sensor noise, environmental ambiguity, and viewpoint variation conditions. RANSAC is often used in the context of loop closures for geometric model fitting in the presence of outliers. However, this approach may fail, leading to map inconsistency. We introduce a novel deterministic algorithm, CliReg, for loop closure validation that replaces RANSAC verification with a maximal clique search over a compatibility graph of feature correspondences. This formulation avoids random sampling and increases robustness in the presence of noise and outliers. We integrated our approach into a real- time pipeline employing binary 3D descriptors and a Hamming distance embedding binary search tree-based matching. We evaluated it on multiple real-world datasets featuring diverse LiDAR sensors. The results demonstrate that our proposed technique consistently achieves a lower pose error and more reliable loop closures than RANSAC, especially in sparse or ambiguous conditions. Additional experiments on 2D projection-based maps confirm its generality across spatial domains, making our approach a robust and efficient alternative for loop closure detection.

</details>


### [225] [ROScopter: A Multirotor Autopilot based on ROSflight 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05404)
*Jacob Moore,Ian Reid,Phil Tokumaru,Tim McLain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROScopter是一个为研究人员设计的轻量级多旋翼自动驾驶仪，基于ROS 2架构，旨在加速研究代码的仿真和硬件测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仪系统通常代码复杂、难以理解和修改，限制了研究代码的快速开发和测试。需要一种既易于理解又便于修改的架构来加速研究代码的仿真和硬件验证

Method: 设计基于ROS 2的模块化架构，完全在机载飞行计算机上运行，与ROSflight 2.0接口兼容。系统支持在仿真环境和硬件环境中测试应用代码，提供标准化的开发框架

Result: ROScopter在基本航点跟踪任务中实现了与最先进自动驾驶仪相似的性能，但代码库显著减少且更具模块化。硬件测试验证了系统的可行性和性能

Conclusion: ROScopter为研究人员提供了一个高效、模块化且易于修改的自动驾驶平台，能够显著加速研究代码的开发和测试过程，在保持性能的同时简化了系统复杂度

Abstract: ROScopter is a lean multirotor autopilot built for researchers. ROScopter seeks to accelerate simulation and hardware testing of research code with an architecture that is both easy to understand and simple to modify. ROScopter is designed to interface with ROSflight 2.0 and runs entirely on an onboard flight computer, leveraging the features of ROS 2 to improve modularity. This work describes the architecture of ROScopter and how it can be used to test application code in both simulated and hardware environments. Hardware results of the default ROScopter behavior are presented, showing that ROScopter achieves similar performance to another state-of-the-art autopilot for basic waypoint-following maneuvers, but with a significantly reduced and more modular code-base.

</details>


### [226] [PhysiFlow: Physics-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body VLA via Multi-Brain Latent Flow Matching and Robust Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05410)
*Weikai Qin,Sichen Wu,Ci Chen,Mengfan Liu,Linxi Feng,Xinru Cui,Haoqi Han,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义-运动意图引导的物理感知多脑VLA框架，用于人形机器人全身控制，解决现有方法VLA推理效率低、缺乏有效语义引导导致动态肢体协调任务不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人形机器人控制领域，视觉-语言-动作（VLA）与全身控制的融合对于语义引导的现实任务执行至关重要。现有方法面临VLA推理效率低或缺乏对全身控制的有效语义引导的挑战，导致动态肢体协调任务不稳定。

Method: 提出语义-运动意图引导的物理感知多脑VLA框架，用于人形机器人全身控制。该框架整合了语义理解和物理约束，通过多脑架构实现高效的VLA推理和有效的语义引导。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够实现人形机器人可靠的视觉-语言引导的全身协调控制。

Conclusion: 提出的语义-运动意图引导的物理感知多脑VLA框架有效解决了现有方法的局限性，为人形机器人提供了稳定可靠的视觉-语言引导全身控制能力。

Abstract: In the domain of humanoid robot control, the fusion of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) with whole-body control is essential for semantically guided execution of real-world tasks. However, existing methods encounter challenges in terms of low VLA inference efficiency or an absence of effective semantic guidance for whole-body control, resulting in instability in dynamic limb-coordinated tasks. To bridge this gap, we present a semantic-motion intent guided, physics-aware multi-brain VLA framework for humanoid whole-body control. A series of experiments was conducted to evaluate the performance of the proposed framework. The experimental results demonstrated that the framework enabled reliable vision-language-guided full-body coordination for humanoid robots.

</details>


### [227] [Residual RL--MPC for Robust Microrobotic Cell Pushing Under Time-Varying Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05448)
*Yanda Yang,Sambeeta Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合控制器，结合MPC与SAC训练的残差策略，用于微流控环境中磁驱动机器人的细胞推操作，提高非稳态流下的鲁棒性和跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微流控环境中的接触式微操作具有挑战性，因为微小扰动会破坏推动接触并引起大的横向漂移。需要开发在时变泊肃叶流下能稳定跟踪参考曲线的控制方法。

Method: 提出混合控制器：在名义MPC基础上增加由SAC训练的残差策略。策略输出有界的2D速度修正，采用接触门控机制，仅在机器人-细胞接触时应用残差动作，保持可靠的接近行为并稳定学习过程。

Result: 实验显示在非稳态流下，相比纯MPC和PID控制器，混合控制器在鲁棒性和跟踪精度方面均有提升。方法能从三叶草训练曲线泛化到未见过的圆形和方形轨迹。残差边界扫描确定了中间修正限制为最佳权衡点。

Conclusion: 接触门控的混合控制器有效提升了微流控环境中磁驱动机器人细胞推操作的性能，在非稳态流下实现了更好的鲁棒性和跟踪精度，并展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich micromanipulation in microfluidic flow is challenging because small disturbances can break pushing contact and induce large lateral drift. We study planar cell pushing with a magnetic rolling microrobot that tracks a waypoint-sampled reference curve under time-varying Poiseuille flow. We propose a hybrid controller that augments a nominal MPC with a learned residual policy trained by SAC. The policy outputs a bounded 2D velocity correction that is contact-gated, so residual actions are applied only during robot--cell contact, preserving reliable approach behavior and stabilizing learning. All methods share the same actuation interface and speed envelope for fair comparisons. Experiments show improved robustness and tracking accuracy over pure MPC and PID under nonstationary flow, with generalization from a clover training curve to unseen circle and square trajectories. A residual-bound sweep identifies an intermediate correction limit as the best trade-off, which we use in all benchmarks.

</details>


### [228] [Observing and Controlling Features in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05487)
*Hugo Buurmeijer,Carmen Amo Alonso,Aiden Swann,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过特征可观测性和特征可控性概念来弥合大型语言模型与视觉-语言-动作模型在可解释性方面的差距，展示了轻量级线性干预如何可靠地引导机器人行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）在具身智能方面取得了显著进展，但其架构比大型语言模型（LLMs）更复杂，包含多模态输入/输出以及混合的transformer和扩散头。这使得LLMs中的机制可解释性洞察无法直接迁移到VLAs中，因此需要弥合这一差距

Method: 提出并分析两个核心概念：特征可观测性和特征可控性。首先研究在表示空间中线性编码的特征，展示如何通过线性分类器观察这些特征。然后使用基于最优控制的最小线性干预，准确放置内部表示并引导VLA输出到期望区域

Result: 结果表明，有针对性的轻量级干预可以可靠地引导机器人行为，同时保持闭环能力。在不同VLA架构（π_{0.5}和OpenVLA）的仿真实验中证明，VLAs具有可解释的内部结构，适合在线适应而无需微调，能够实现与用户偏好和任务需求的实时对齐

Conclusion: VLAs具有可解释的内部结构，可以通过轻量级线性干预进行在线适应，无需微调即可实现实时对齐，为VLA的可解释性和可控性提供了新方法

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) have shown remarkable progress towards embodied intelligence. While their architecture partially resembles that of Large Language Models (LLMs), VLAs exhibit higher complexity due to their multi-modal inputs/outputs and often hybrid nature of transformer and diffusion heads. This is part of the reason why insights from mechanistic interpretability in LLMs, which explain how the internal model representations relate to their output behavior, do not trivially transfer to VLA counterparts. In this work, we propose to close this gap by introducing and analyzing two main concepts: feature-observability and feature-controllability. In particular, we first study features that are linearly encoded in representation space, and show how they can be observed by means of a linear classifier. Then, we use a minimal linear intervention grounded in optimal control to accurately place internal representations and steer the VLA's output towards a desired region. Our results show that targeted, lightweight interventions can reliably steer a robot's behavior while preserving closed-loop capabilities. We demonstrate on different VLA architectures ($π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA) through simulation experiments that VLAs possess interpretable internal structure amenable to online adaptation without fine-tuning, enabling real-time alignment with user preferences and task requirements.

</details>


### [229] [cuRoboV2: Dynamics-Aware Motion Generation with Depth-Fused Distance Fields for High-DoF Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05493)
*Balakumar Sundaralingam,Adithyavairavan Murali,Stan Birchfield*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: cuRoboV2是一个统一的机器人运动生成框架，通过B样条轨迹优化、GPU原生感知流水线和可扩展的全身计算，实现了从单臂机械手到完整人形机器人的动态感知运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人运动生成方法存在碎片化问题：快速规划器输出物理不可执行的轨迹，反应式控制器难以处理高保真感知，现有求解器无法处理高自由度系统。需要统一的、动态感知的运动生成框架。

Method: 提出三个关键创新：1) B样条轨迹优化，确保平滑性和扭矩限制；2) GPU原生的TSDF/ESDF感知流水线，生成覆盖整个工作空间的密集符号距离场；3) 可扩展的GPU原生全身计算，包括拓扑感知运动学、可微逆动力学和map-reduce自碰撞检测。

Result: 在3kg负载下达到99.7%成功率（基线仅72-77%），48自由度人形机器人上实现99.6%无碰撞逆运动学（先前方法完全失败），重定向约束满足率达到89.5%（PyRoki为61%）。感知流水线比最先进方法快10倍且内存减少8倍，全身计算实现61倍加速。

Conclusion: cuRoboV2提供了一个统一的、动态感知的运动生成堆栈，能够从单臂机械手扩展到完整人形机器人。代码库的重新设计还展示了良好结构的机器人代码可以解锁高效的人-LLM协作。

Abstract: Effective robot autonomy requires motion generation that is safe, feasible, and reactive. Current methods are fragmented: fast planners output physically unexecutable trajectories, reactive controllers struggle with high-fidelity perception, and existing solvers fail on high-DoF systems. We present cuRoboV2, a unified framework with three key innovations: (1) B-spline trajectory optimization that enforces smoothness and torque limits; (2) a GPU-native TSDF/ESDF perception pipeline that generates dense signed distance fields covering the full workspace, unlike existing methods that only provide distances within sparsely allocated blocks, up to 10x faster and in 8x less memory than the state-of-the-art at manipulation scale, with up to 99% collision recall; and (3) scalable GPU-native whole-body computation, namely topology-aware kinematics, differentiable inverse dynamics, and map-reduce self-collision, that achieves up to 61x speedup while also extending to high-DoF humanoids (where previous GPU implementations fail).
